(13:39) gp > simulate_log_mass(sample_size, mean_val, std_val) = {
  my(v, i, pair);
  v = vector(sample_size);
  for (i=1, floor(sample_size/2),
    pair = box_muller();
    v[2*i-1] = mean_val + std_val * pair[1];
    if (2*i <= sample_size, v[2*i] = mean_val + std_val * pair[2]);
  );
  if (sample_size % 2 == 1,

  );
  v
}

(14:12) gp > \\ Define simulate_log_mass function with different parameter names to avoid conflicts
(14:12) gp > simulate_log_mass(sample_size, mean_val, std_val) = {
  my(v, i, pair);
  v = vector(sample_size);
  for (i=1, floor(sample_size/2),
    pair = box_muller();
    v[2*i-1] = mean_val + std_val * pair[1];
    if (2*i <= sample_size, v[2*i] = mean_val + std_val * pair[2]);
  );
  if (sample_size % 2 == 1,
    pair = box_muller();
    v[sample_size] = mean_val + std_val * pair[1];
  );
v
}

(14:12) gp > compute_K_theory(logmass_vec) = {
  my(n = length(logmass_vec), masses, sorted_masses, m0, sum_m_over_m0, sum_m0_over_m, k);
  masses = vector(n, i, 10^logmass_vec[i]);

  m0 = sorted_masses[floor((n+1)/2)];
  sum_m_over_m0 = sum(i=1, n, masses[i] / m0);
  sum_m0_over_m = sum(i=1, n, m0 / masses[i]);
  k = sum_m_over_m0 / sum_m0_over_m;
  k
}

(14:21) gp > compute_K_theory(logmass_vec) = {
  my(n = length(logmass_vec), masses, sorted_masses, m0, sum_m_over_m0, sum_m0_over_m, k);
  masses = vector(n, i, 10^logmass_vec[i]);
  sorted_masses = vecsort(masses);
  m0 = sorted_masses[floor((n+1)/2)];
  sum_m_over_m0 = sum(i=1, n, masses[i] / m0);
  sum_m0_over_m = sum(i=1, n, m0 / masses[i]);
  k = sum_m_over_m0 / sum_m0_over_m;
  k
}

(14:21) gp > compute_kurtosis(vec) = {
  my(n = length(vec), mean, m4, variance);
  mean = sum(i=1, n, vec[i]) / n;
  m4 = sum(i=1, n, (vec[i] - mean)^4) / n;
  variance = sum(i=1, n, (vec[i] - mean)^2) / n;
  m4 / variance^2
}
